# Audit — 2026-06-13

## 1. Audit header

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Date | 2026-06-13 |
| Commit | `1f52db38d468b907eb267a65763c1b6760c2db11` (develop tip; audited via a detached read-only worktree snapshot) |
| Repo size | 1,361 tracked files — 765 `.py`, 267 `.ts`, 186 `.tsx`, 84 `.md` |
| Structure | Python 3.11 FastAPI backend (`src/groundmodel/` — api / application / domain / infrastructure / shared, async SQLAlchemy + asyncpg, Alembic, Polars), Electron + React + TypeScript desktop (`desktop/` — main / preload / renderer), pytest (unit / integration / e2e / benchmarks) + Vitest + Playwright, PowerShell tooling under `scripts/`, docs under `docs/` |
| Method | 13 independent parallel reviewer subagents (architecture, security, correctness, dead code, testing, performance, dependencies, maintainability, DRY, KISS/YAGNI, naming, observability, documentation), read-only; findings deduplicated and severity-reconciled by the orchestrator |
| Prior audit | `docs/superpowers/2026-06-11-codebase-audit.md` — its 6 Phase-0 security items were independently re-verified (5 fixed, 1 open; see SEC appendix) |

## 2. Executive summary

**Overall health: good for a pre-revenue solo project, with one concentrated wound.** The clean-architecture boundary holds in the domain layer, Electron hardening is genuinely strong (contextIsolation/sandbox/CSP/IPC path-allowlists/defusedxml all verified healthy), the security posture improved sharply since the 2026-06-11 audit (5 of 6 Phase-0 items fixed), commit hygiene is 100% conventional-commits, and dependency lockfiles are present and consistent.

**Dominant risk themes:**

1. **Retirement shrapnel from the body-extent removal (2026-06-06).** The single most important cluster: two live right-click gestures ("Open end", "Pinch out") and the lens-inspector "Name this body" save all POST to a backend route that no longer exists — they 404 silently in production. Around them sit a vestigial error class, four dead client methods, always-zero response fields, a no-op e2e helper, and ~12 stale lens/extent references in docstrings. The backend retirement was thorough; the frontend sweep was not.
2. **Concurrency and partial-commit gaps in the async backend.** A lost-update race on `seeded_formation_contact_ids`, an SSE sentinel that never arrives if the background task dies, swallowed per-item exceptions that still commit partially-staged session state, and a cross-project `formation_id` acceptance on bulk layer creation.
3. **The section-fill render path recomputes its geometry engine on every pan/zoom frame** (LCS + polygon construction keyed on screen-transform callbacks), with no regression test guarding the split.
4. **Documentation has fallen two phases behind the code.** README still describes Phase 2; CLAUDE.md is 59% inline debt history (44.9 kB of its 76 kB); api.md documents a phantom endpoint and misses ~17 live ones; 97% of router endpoints lack docstrings.
5. **Pre-distribution loose ends:** DevTools/reload in the packaged menu, Postgres bound to `0.0.0.0:5433` with default credentials, no React error boundary or main-process exception handlers, unbounded `backend.log`, Hippocratic-licensed `react-leaflet` in a commercial product, floating GitHub-Action tags.

**Finding counts after dedup and reconciliation (121 findings):**

| Severity | Count |
|---|---|
| Critical | 0 |
| High | 14 |
| Medium | 42 |
| Low | 36 |
| Informational | 29 |

No exploitable-security or data-loss-class Critical findings at the current threat model (local desktop, localhost-bound backend, pre-distribution). Two reviewer-rated "Critical" items were reconciled down (DEAD-01: broken feature, not data loss; OBS-01: observability gap).

## 6. Major refactors (>1 week)

- **Repository extraction for the application layer** (ARCH-04, debt #106): ~55 use cases import SQLAlchemy/ORM directly. Phased: repositories per aggregate, extract on demand when files are touched. Do not big-bang.
- **Transaction-ownership rule** (ARCH-06): use cases flush, boundary commits (UnitOfWork). Apply to new code immediately; backfill the 45 commit sites opportunistically.
- **Debt #201 god-component decomposition** (MAINT-01/09): ContactsLayer / SectionPanel / useGeologicalModel / geology client split per the existing plan doc; per-axis props; verb-grouped sub-routers + sub-clients to cut the 12-file change amplification for a new litho endpoint.
- **Router docstring backfill** (DOC-05): 113 endpoints; template-driven, geology/ first.
- **English canonical lithology vocabulary** (NAME-11 — disputed, see appendix): only if the team decides domain branching should not be on raw Dutch DOV tokens; the store-raw-tokens rule sanctions the current state, so this is a deliberate-decision item, not automatic debt.

## 7. Remediation roadmap

Ordered to maximise risk reduction per unit effort; items within a phase are independent.

1. **Phase 0 — pre-coworker-binary blockers (~3 days, all quick wins).** Dead-gesture removal (Top-1/2) → packaged-menu DevTools gating → Postgres loopback bind → Electron patch bump + exact pin → main-process exception handlers + backend-failed IPC → ErrorBoundary (start) → README rewrite. *Rationale: everything a coworker would hit or expose in week one.*
2. **Phase 1 — correctness under concurrency (~1 week).** COR-01 sentinel, COR-02 atomic seeded-ids, COR-03 formation ownership, COR-04/06 savepoints, COR-08/09/10 epsilon + scope hygiene. Gate each with the matching test (TEST-05 pattern). *Depends on nothing; unblocks trustworthy bulk flows.*
3. **Phase 2 — section-editor performance + its guard rails (~1 week).** PERF-01 memo split (do TEST-04's call-count test first, watch it fail, then split), PERF-02 map lookup, PERF-03/04 backend batching, PERF-08 React.memo on overlays. *Do after Phase 1 so geometry refactors land on tested fill logic (TEST-01 first).*
4. **Phase 3 — retirement-shrapnel + naming sweep (~3 days).** §8 deletions, lens→body rename batch (NAME-01..08), DRY-01 scope-guard migration, dead-field removal, stale docstrings (DEAD-10/11, DOC-08). *Cheap, large readability payoff, zero behaviour change — ideal between feature pushes.*
5. **Phase 4 — dependency + supply chain (~1 week, partly waiting on decisions).** react-leaflet replacement (legal decision first), GH-Action SHA pins, docker digest pin, python upper bounds, lockfile-drift CI check, Electron support-window verdict.
6. **Phase 5 — docs + structure (ongoing).** CLAUDE.md prune, api.md/architecture.md refresh, docstring backfill (routers last), MAINT file splits, debt #201 execution. *Schedule the CLAUDE.md prune early in this phase — it pays back on every future AI-assisted session.*

Dependencies worth noting: TEST-01 before PERF-01 (don't refactor the fill path untested); the NAME-09 column rename and ARCH-08 rename must land while backwards-compat is still suspended; DEP-02 needs an owner license-risk decision before engineering effort.

## 9. Full findings appendix

Findings below are deduplicated; severities are the reconciled values (where the orchestrator changed a reviewer's rating, the original is noted). Locations cite the audited snapshot at commit `1f52db38`.

### 9.1 Architecture (ARCH)

- **ID**: ARCH-01 — **Severity**: High — **Location**: `desktop/src/renderer/src/shell/stages/sections/SectionsCanvas.tsx:745–848` (`handleOpenEndLayer`, `handlePinchOutLayer`); `desktop/src/renderer/src/api/clients/geology.ts:1666–1703` (`claimBodyExtent`) — **Issue**: Both gestures POST to `/geology/models/{mid}/bodies`, deleted in the 2026-06-06 body-extent retirement; every invocation 404s, caught as a generic warn — silent dead feature. *(Merged with DEAD-01/-03.)* — **Why it matters**: Two named UI verbs are non-functional with zero feedback; coworker-binary blocker. — **Remediation**: Remove handlers + toolbar/menu wiring + `claimBodyExtent` + `ProvenanceViolationError`; grep all importers. — **Effort**: Quick win — **LLM-suspect**: yes
- **ID**: ARCH-02 — **Severity**: Medium (reviewers split High/Medium) — **Location**: `desktop/package.json:44` (`"electron": "^42.0.1"`) — **Issue**: Pinned at the *first* patch of the 42 line via caret; reviewers disagreed on whether 42 is inside Electron's 3-major support window as of 2026-06 (knowledge-cutoff limits). — **Why it matters**: Any Chromium CVE patched in later 42.x is absent from builds; if 42 is out-of-window there is no patch path at all. — **Remediation**: Verify the support window on releases.electronjs.org; `npm update electron` to latest 42.x; pin exact; add a pre-distribution bump checklist item. — **Effort**: Quick win (verify+patch) / Medium (major upgrade if needed) — **LLM-suspect**: no
- **ID**: ARCH-03 — **Severity**: High — **Location**: `desktop/src/renderer/src/components/sections/PartitionFillOverlay.tsx:621–942` (`screenRegions` memo) — **Issue**: Memo deps include `levelToScreen` / `alongToScreenX` / `plotTop` / `plotBottom` (recreated per pan/zoom), so `computeSectionFill` (~2,081-line LCS + polygon engine) reruns every frame. *(Merged with PERF-01; test gap = TEST-04.)* — **Why it matters**: Main-thread jank on every wheel tick at realistic section sizes; primary interactive-perf risk pre-distribution. — **Remediation**: Two-layer memo: data-keyed geometry, transform-keyed screen projection. — **Effort**: Medium — **LLM-suspect**: yes
- **ID**: ARCH-04 — **Severity**: Medium — **Location**: ~55 of ~73 files in `src/groundmodel/application/` (e.g. `accept_contact_correlations.py:36–44`) — **Issue**: Application layer imports SQLAlchemy + concrete ORM models throughout (debt #106, confirmed growing). — **Why it matters**: ORM changes fan out across 55 files; use-case unit tests need a live session. — **Remediation**: Phased repository extraction per aggregate; extract on touch, never big-bang. — **Effort**: Major — **LLM-suspect**: no
- **ID**: ARCH-05 — **Severity**: Medium — **Location**: `src/groundmodel/api/routers/geology/bodies.py:95–185` (`_geo_labels_by_body`; 17 `session.execute` calls in the router) — **Issue**: Three-query domain-join logic lives in an API router helper. — **Why it matters**: Untestable without HTTP stack; will be duplicated by the export path. — **Remediation**: Extract a `GetBodyGeoLabels` use case. — **Effort**: Quick win — **LLM-suspect**: yes
- **ID**: ARCH-06 — **Severity**: Medium — **Location**: 28 `session.commit()` sites across routers + 45 across use cases; `commit: bool` param on ~12 use cases — **Issue**: Split transaction ownership; orchestrating two non-`commit=False` use cases silently yields partial commits. — **Why it matters**: Atomicity is convention-dependent, unreadable in audit. — **Remediation**: Rule: use cases flush, boundary commits (UnitOfWork); new code immediately, backfill opportunistically. — **Effort**: Major (full backfill) / Medium (rule + new code) — **LLM-suspect**: yes
- **ID**: ARCH-07 — **Severity**: Medium — **Location**: `src/groundmodel/application/batch_update_litho_layers.py:30–36, 85–86` — **Issue**: `BatchUpdateResult.layers` carries raw ORM rows to the API (latent `MissingGreenlet` per the project's own CLAUDE.md trap); unconstrained `setattr(row, key, value)` patch loop writes any dict key. — **Why it matters**: Future relationship + stale eager-load = runtime 500; setattr bypasses invariants for unexpected keys. — **Remediation**: Pydantic objects in results; `PATCHABLE_FIELDS` allowlist. — **Effort**: Quick win — **LLM-suspect**: yes
- **ID**: ARCH-08 — **Severity**: Low — **Location**: `litho_contact_correlation_edge_orm.py:134–150` — **Issue**: `contact_a_id`/`contact_b_id` FK to `litho_layer.id`, not contacts; a load-bearing comment is the only guard. — **Why it matters**: Eight consuming use cases; any naive navigation gets the wrong entity. — **Remediation**: Rename to `layer_a_id`/`layer_b_id` (migration + ORM + use cases + TS) while compat is suspended. — **Effort**: Medium — **LLM-suspect**: no
- **ID**: ARCH-09 — **Severity**: Low — **Location**: `src/groundmodel/api/routers/jobs.py:133–163` (`DELETE /jobs/{job_id}`) — **Issue**: No project scope on job cancel. — **Why it matters**: Mitigated by UUIDs + loopback today; IDOR the day anything is network-reachable. — **Remediation**: Scope-guard via the job's `project_id`. — **Effort**: Quick win — **LLM-suspect**: no
- **ID**: ARCH-10 — **Severity**: Informational — **Location**: `domain/services/tin_surface.py:51` (`from scipy.spatial import Delaunay`) — **Issue**: Heavy C-extension dependency inside the domain layer (grey area vs the no-frameworks rule). — **Why it matters**: Precedent risk more than concrete harm. — **Remediation**: Defer; wrap behind a protocol only if a second impl or pure-python testing need appears. — **Effort**: Medium (if ever) — **LLM-suspect**: no

### 9.2 Security (SEC)

Prior-audit re-verification: migration f-string injection **FIXED**; batch IDOR **FIXED**; DSN echo **FIXED**; `isInAppNavigation` file:// wildcard **FIXED**; SECRET_KEY default **guarded for non-dev** (warning-only in dev); Electron 42.0.1 **OPEN** (see ARCH-02). Verified healthy: defusedxml everywhere on DOV paths; contextIsolation/sandbox/nodeIntegration correct; IPC file paths dialog-allowlisted; CORS + TrustedHost close DNS-rebinding; bundle size caps + 256 MiB body limit; parameterized runtime SQL; permkey-regex cache paths; hardcoded outbound URLs (no SSRF); project-scope guards present on all checked by-id endpoints; preload surface enumerated.

- **ID**: SEC-01 — **Severity**: Medium — **Location**: `desktop/src/main/index.ts:409` — **Issue**: `toggleDevTools` + `reload` menu roles (and F12/Ctrl+Shift+I accelerators) unconditional in packaged builds. — **Why it matters**: Primary disclosure surface for a binary recipient (all API responses, NDA-adjacent geometry); debt #200 remainder. — **Remediation**: Gate on `!app.isPackaged`; block the accelerator via `before-input-event`. — **Effort**: Quick win — **LLM-suspect**: no
- **ID**: SEC-02 — **Severity**: Low (reviewer Medium) — **Location**: `update_body_color_request.py:26` — **Issue**: `display_color` validated only by `max_length=20` — any string stored and echoed to renderer/Cesium. — **Why it matters**: Correctness/boundary hygiene more than exploit (local app, no SSR). — **Remediation**: `pattern=r'^#[0-9a-fA-F]{3,8}$'`. — **Effort**: Quick win — **LLM-suspect**: no
- **ID**: SEC-03 — **Severity**: Low (reviewer Medium) — **Location**: `migrations/versions/a1c2d3e4f5a6_schema_invariant_checks.py:85–168`; same pattern in `11878f8a5732`, `f7a8b9c0d1e2` — **Issue**: f-string SQL from compile-time-constant tuples — safe today, but re-teaches the exact idiom the prior audit's real injection used. *(Merged with COR-05, DRY-08.)* — **Why it matters**: Pattern propagation risk into a future migration with a non-constant value. — **Remediation**: Bind params via `sa.text(...).bindparams(...)`; add a migration-SQL rule note to CLAUDE.md. — **Effort**: Quick win — **LLM-suspect**: yes
- **ID**: SEC-04 — **Severity**: Medium — **Location**: `desktop/package.json:44`, lockfile — **Issue**: Lockfile freezes the first 42.x patch — later-patched Chromium CVEs absent. *(See ARCH-02 / DEP-01 for the support-window question.)* — **Remediation**: Update within range, re-run e2e, pin exact, pre-distribution bump rule. — **Effort**: Quick win — **LLM-suspect**: no
- **ID**: SEC-05 — **Severity**: Medium — **Location**: `shared/config.py:39` (default DSN) + `docker-compose.yml` (`5433:5432` → 0.0.0.0) — **Issue**: Known default password on a LAN-exposed port. — **Why it matters**: Shared-office machine = readable project DB; worsens at coworker deployment. — **Remediation**: `127.0.0.1:5433:5432`; random password at first run under the bundled-Postgres plan (debt #2). — **Effort**: Quick win — **LLM-suspect**: no
- **ID**: SEC-06 — **Severity**: Low — **Location**: `api/routers/dem.py:34–100` — **Issue**: Unauthenticated DEM sampler can be driven by any localhost-origin page (CORS regex allows `http://localhost:*`) → third-party WCS quota burn; no data exposure. — **Remediation**: Defer to the auth phase (debt #3/#4). — **Effort**: N/A — **LLM-suspect**: no
- **ID**: SEC-07 — **Severity**: Low — **Location**: `desktop/src/main/index.ts:408` — **Issue**: `reload` role in production menu wipes in-memory UI state without confirmation. — **Remediation**: Gate with SEC-01. — **Effort**: Quick win — **LLM-suspect**: no
- **ID**: SEC-08 — **Severity**: Low — **Location**: `api/routers/project.py:307–311` + `repositories/project_bundle.py` — **Issue**: Bundle children are `dict[str, Any]`; field-level type/sanity validation deferred to ORM/DB — crafted bundles can land odd-but-typed values (e.g. non-monotonic depths). — **Why it matters**: Worst case today is failed restore or corrupt-looking viz, not compromise; matters more once .strix files are shared between coworkers. — **Remediation**: Restore-time numeric sanity pass (monotonic depths, non-negative qc). — **Effort**: Medium — **LLM-suspect**: no

### 9.3 Correctness (COR)

- **ID**: COR-01 — **Severity**: High — **Location**: `application/job_registry.py:98` — **Issue**: `asyncio.create_task` with no done-callback; a crashed job coroutine can leave the SSE queue without its `None` sentinel → consumers block forever, progress UI freezes. — **Why it matters**: Hung import dialogs with no recovery path. — **Remediation**: `add_done_callback` enqueuing the sentinel on exception, or unconditional try/finally sentinel. — **Effort**: Quick win (fix) / Medium (with test) — **LLM-suspect**: yes
- **ID**: COR-02 — **Severity**: High — **Location**: `repositories/cross_section_repository.py:177–180` — **Issue**: Python-side read-union-write of `seeded_formation_contact_ids` with no lock/atomic SQL — concurrent accepts clobber each other (bulk flows fire concurrent requests). — **Why it matters**: Accepted contacts silently disappear from the section (explicit-content invariant broken) — matches historic "nothing appears" bug class. — **Remediation**: Single atomic SQL array-union UPDATE, or `pg_advisory_xact_lock(section_id)` (pattern already exists in `SaveBodyBoundary`). — **Effort**: Medium — **LLM-suspect**: yes
- **ID**: COR-03 — **Severity**: High (security-adjacent) — **Location**: `application/bulk_create_litho_layers.py:80` — **Issue**: `formation_id` from the payload is used without verifying its `app_project_id` — cross-project layer creation. — **Why it matters**: Cross-project contamination invisible to both owners; class of bug the batch-IDOR fix addressed elsewhere. — **Remediation**: Ownership check on the formation; audit sibling bulk endpoints. — **Effort**: Quick win — **LLM-suspect**: yes
- **ID**: COR-04 — **Severity**: Medium — **Location**: `application/recompute_all_absences_for_model.py:270–294` — **Issue**: Per-formation `except (ValueError, LookupError)` continues, but partially-staged ORM state from the failed formation rides the single final commit — no savepoint. — **Why it matters**: Absence table can hold partial inferences the log says were skipped. — **Remediation**: `begin_nested()` per formation, rollback on exception. — **Effort**: Medium — **LLM-suspect**: yes
- **ID**: COR-05 — **Severity**: Low — *Merged into SEC-03 (migration f-string pattern).*
- **ID**: COR-06 — **Severity**: Medium — **Location**: `application/materialise_body_companions.py:191` — **Issue**: Broad `except Exception` per companion; partial flushes survive into the caller's commit; only a counter records the failure. — **Remediation**: Savepoint per companion (same as COR-04). — **Effort**: Medium — **LLM-suspect**: yes
- **ID**: COR-07 — **Severity**: Low (reviewer Medium) — **Location**: `api/main.py` lifespan `_sweep_stale_bodies` (~43–101) — **Issue**: Shared session across per-model commits; identity-map staleness after a per-model rollback is configuration-dependent — structurally unsound even if currently benign. — **Remediation**: Fresh session (or explicit `expire_all`) per model. — **Effort**: Quick win — **LLM-suspect**: no
- **ID**: COR-08 — **Severity**: Low — **Location**: `application/update_formation_contact.py:369,397` — **Issue**: Exact float equality as the bidirectional litho↔geo propagation cycle-breaker; one ULP of drift (mm-rounding/ORM round-trip) defeats the guard. — **Remediation**: ε ≥ 0.0005 m. — **Effort**: Quick win — **LLM-suspect**: yes
- **ID**: COR-09 — **Severity**: Low — **Location**: `application/accept_litho_suggestions.py:1147` — **Issue**: SQL `depth_top_m == :value` exact-double dedup in `_find_or_create_layer` — clean-decimal vs arithmetic-derived values double-insert near-identical layers. — **Remediation**: mm-rounded comparison or BETWEEN ±ε. — **Effort**: Quick win — **LLM-suspect**: yes
- **ID**: COR-10 — **Severity**: Low — **Location**: `application/update_formation_contact.py:391–394` — **Issue**: geo→litho propagation query lacks the defence-in-depth `app_project_id` filter every sibling query carries (FK saves it today). — **Remediation**: Add the scope filter. — **Effort**: Quick win — **LLM-suspect**: no
- **ID**: COR-11 — **Severity**: Low — **Location**: `components/sections/useSectionData.ts:174,320,378` — **Issue**: Three `eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps` without the CLAUDE.md-mandated `// reason:` comment — the exact S7-incident bug class. — **Remediation**: Add reasons or fix the dep arrays. — **Effort**: Quick win — **LLM-suspect**: no

Per-gap/per-side asymmetry (band-left + pinch-right), 0-corr-both-sides, degenerate 0/1-column cases all uncovered. — **Why it matters**: Multi-gap regressions invisible to CI; wrong terminations cascade into 3D meshes. — **Remediation**: 4–6 `sf()` tests incl. `assertTiles` on each. — **Effort**: Quick win — **LLM-suspect**: no

### 9.8 Maintainability (MAINT)

### 9.12 Logging / observability (OBS)

Reviewer verdict: backend import/parse/service logging genuinely strong per the DoD; gaps concentrate in silent swallows, the renderer, and crash surfaces.

- **ID**: OBS-01 — **Severity**: Medium (reviewer Critical — observability, not data loss) — **Location**: `application/import_cpt_dov.py:35–52` (`_fire_pins`: `except Exception: pass  # noqa: BLE001`) — **Issue**: SSE callback failures discarded silently — stalled progress bar is indistinguishable from success. — **Remediation**: One-line `self.warning(...)`; then grep the application layer for siblings (OBS-11). — **Effort**: Quick win — **LLM-suspect**: yes
- **ID**: OBS-02 — **Severity**: Medium (reviewer High) — **Location**: `desktop/src/main/index.ts` — **Issue**: No `process.on('uncaughtException'/'unhandledRejection')`; main-process death leaves no log line (Crashpad minidumps exist but upload is off, debt #16). — **Remediation**: Two handlers writing to the stdout already routed to backend.log. — **Effort**: Quick win — **LLM-suspect**: yes
- **ID**: OBS-03 — **Severity**: High — **Location**: `desktop/src/renderer/src/` — no ErrorBoundary, no `window.onerror`, no `unhandledrejection` listener — **Issue**: Any render exception in the geometry-heavy components = blank app, zero trace in packaged builds (`render-process-gone` doesn't fire for React errors). — **Remediation**: Top-level boundary + global rejection handler + one render-error test. — **Effort**: Medium — **LLM-suspect**: yes
- **ID**: OBS-04 — **Severity**: Medium (reviewer High) — **Location**: `main/index.ts` — `createWriteStream(logPath, { flags: 'a' })` — **Issue**: backend.log grows unbounded across sessions in packaged builds. — **Remediation**: Rotating stream (5 MB × 3) + surface the path (OBS-10). — **Effort**: Quick win — **LLM-suspect**: no
- **ID**: OBS-05 — **Severity**: Medium (reviewer High) — **Location**: `PartitionFillOverlay.tsx` (1,667 lines) + `partitionFill.ts` — zero log calls — **Issue**: The highest-complexity frontend path is undiagnosable from logs ("section fill looks wrong" → nothing). — **Remediation**: ~10 `log.debug` decision points (entry counts, per-unit `classifyTermination`, carve entry). — **Effort**: Quick win — **LLM-suspect**: yes
- **ID**: OBS-06 — **Severity**: Medium — **Location**: `api/clients/shared.ts` (error-only logging); `geology.ts` (2 of 73 functions log) — **Issue**: No happy-path API trace — "was the call even fired?" unanswerable. — **Remediation**: One `log.debug(method, path)` in the shared helpers; payload-key debug on the critical verbs. — **Effort**: Quick win — **LLM-suspect**: yes
- **ID**: OBS-07 — **Severity**: Medium — **Location**: `useGeologicalModelOps.ts`, `useLithoSuggestions.ts` ("… see console" toasts; `log.debug` in catch blocks) — **Issue**: Production console is empty where the toast points (debug suppressed). — **Remediation**: `log.error` in catches; toast copy → "Help → Show Log File". — **Effort**: Quick win — **LLM-suspect**: yes
- **ID**: OBS-08 — **Severity**: Medium — **Location**: `shared.ts` + `api/main.py` — **Issue**: No request-ID correlation between frontend action and backend log lines; concurrent bulk POSTs require timestamp archaeology. — **Remediation**: `X-Request-ID` (crypto.randomUUID) + FastAPI middleware injecting `rid=` into log records. — **Effort**: Medium — **LLM-suspect**: no
- **ID**: OBS-09 — **Severity**: Low (reviewer Medium) — **Location**: `ContactsLayer.tsx` — 5 log calls (all drag-commit: 1945–2072) in 3,820 lines — **Issue**: Snap-candidate selection, clamp activation, fetch counts all silent. — **Remediation**: Three debug points. — **Effort**: Quick win — **LLM-suspect**: yes
- **ID**: OBS-10 — **Severity**: Low — **Location**: `main/index.ts` (logPath never surfaced); no Help-menu item — **Issue**: The log support depends on is invisible to the user who must attach it. — **Remediation**: Help → "Show Log File" (`shell.showItemInFolder`). — **Effort**: Quick win — **LLM-suspect**: no
- **ID**: OBS-11 — **Severity**: Low — **Location**: `application/import_borehole_dov.py` (unverified sibling of OBS-01) — **Issue**: Same `_fire_pins` shape likely copied; verify and fix together. — **Remediation**: Grep `# noqa: BLE001` / `except Exception: pass` across application/. — **Effort**: Quick win — **LLM-suspect**: yes
- **ID**: OBS-12 — **Severity**: Low — **Location**: `api/main.py` `_sweep_stale_bodies` — **Issue**: Per-model WARNINGs but no aggregate "swept N, M failures" exit line (DoD exit-point rule). — **Remediation**: One summary line. — **Effort**: Quick win — **LLM-suspect**: no

- **ID**: DOC-09 — **Severity**: Medium — **Location**: 74/249 application module-level public functions without docstrings; ~60 more non-numpy; worst: `cpt_corrections.calculate_qt_series`, `cpt_interpretation.calculate_ic_series`, `litho_body_assembly.py` (6/6 undocumented), DOV wfs/xml helpers (7/7) — **Issue**: The algorithm-dense, unit-sensitive functions have zero parameter/units documentation. — **Remediation**: Prioritised backfill (corrections/interpretation → body assembly → DOV helpers); consider docstring-presence enforcement in the hook. — **Effort**: Medium — **LLM-suspect**: yes
- **ID**: DOC-10 — **Severity**: Medium (reviewer Low for the runbook part; raised for the README test instruction — note the conftest `_integration`/`_test` guard does fail loudly, so this is misleading-not-destructive) — **Location**: `README.md:83–87`; no CONTRIBUTING.md / troubleshooting runbook — **Issue**: README shows bare `uv run pytest tests/integration` with no wrapper-script mention; common machine failures (port 8765, Node pin, HVCI) live only in private memory files. — **Remediation**: Wrapper-script callout; `docs/troubleshooting.md` with the three known traps; minimal CONTRIBUTING.md → CLAUDE.md pointer. — **Effort**: Quick win — **LLM-suspect**: no

# Audit — 2026-07-12 (redundancy / efficiency)

## 1. Audit header

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Date | 2026-07-12 |
| Commit | `d8694786` (develop tip; read-only review, one fix landed during the audit: the partitionFill NUL byte) |
| Mandate | **Redundancy and inefficiency** — near-duplicate code accreted across AI sessions, dead code from superseded designs, parallel abstractions. NOT a general health audit (that was 2026-06-13). |
| Method | 13 parallel reviewer subagents with **bounded scopes** (be-domain-litho, be-domain-other, be-3d-builders, be-application-litho, be-application-geotech-import, be-infrastructure, be-api, fe-partition-fill, fe-sections, fe-hooks, fe-utils, fe-shell-clients, tooling), lenses weighted AI-accretion / DRY / dead / KISS / perf; every delete/duplicate/superseded claim piped through an **adversarial verifier** told to refute it (independent re-grep incl. dynamic dispatch, side-by-side re-read); 2 further agents re-verified all 121 findings of the 2026-06-13 audit against HEAD. 28 agents, ~5.2M tokens, 1,346 tool calls. |
| Prior audit | 2026-06-13 section above (121 findings). Its companion `docs/superpowers/2026-06-11-codebase-audit.md` was purged 2026-07-12 with the rest of docs/superpowers/ — resolve via git history (docs/superpowers/README.md). |
| Verification | 201 findings: **145 confirmed**, **14 adjusted** (real issue, wrong action/severity — corrected verdict recorded per finding), **0 refuted**, 42 perf/complexity/watch findings carried as static analysis without an adversarial pass (marked `unverified`). |

## 2. Executive summary

**The 2026-06-13 diagnosis ("one concentrated wound") no longer fits — the dominant pattern now is superseded-design generations sitting beside their replacements.** Every major subsystem that went through the build→dogfeed→pivot cycle since May kept its previous generation: the implicit strat-field 3D builder (~290 KB) beside the voxel builder; the C1/C2a/C2b geotech skeleton flow (3 endpoints, ~9 schema files, 3 use cases, 3 client fns) beside the shipped body-centric subdivide path; the `!rewriteB` legacy fill path (~1,100 lines) beside the live rewriteB model; a GeotechnicalUnit CRUD surface beside the model-kind design; a 2,449-line Python port of `partitionFill.ts` beside the TS original it self-documents as lagging. **Verified production-dead or production-unreachable code exceeds ~12,000 lines** — none of it load-bearing, much of it still test-covered (which keeps it green and invisible).

**Highest-risk single finding is not dead code but a testing inversion (FE-PARTITION-FILL-02):** the default vitest suite exercises the *retired* `!rewriteB` fill path — the path production actually runs is only tested when a human manually sets `REWRITE_B=1`. The pre-commit gate is green-lighting changes against behaviour the app never executes. Second is a **bug-adjacent redundancy (BE-APPLICATION-LITHO-01, orchestrator-verified):** the wedge-tip stale-delete query is not section-scoped but runs inside the per-section loop, so on a multi-section project each section's pass deletes the tips the previous section just created, leaving dangling seeded ids — only the last section's tips survive.

**Duplication has a clear epicentre: the live geotech milestone.** The find-or-create formation trio exists in 3 near-verbatim ~250-line copies across the two shipped use cases plus a third variant (debt #224 confirmed and grown); `GeotechnicalCanvas` is a ~350-line admitted-verbatim copy of `LithologyCanvas` (with `GeologyCanvas` carrying a third copy of two blocks); `_resolve_anchor_kinds` has 3 verbatim copies; the nearest-geo-contact loop has 3. In the accretion-per-file league, `partitionFill.ts` grew 2,081 → 6,926 lines (3.3×) since the last audit and contained a raw NUL byte that made ripgrep silently treat the repo's most-edited file as binary (fixed during this audit).

**The dead-client-method problem regrew after #207.** The 2026-06-16 sweep deleted the body-extent client shrapnel; since then ~25 new dead client methods/types accreted (geotech ghost flow, litho-geo link/unlink superseded by layer endpoints, non-streaming DOV imports superseded by SSE, GeotechnicalUnit client, recompute-absence pair, project.list/listArchived/unarchive) — same mechanism, new generation.

**Prior-audit scorecard: of 144 tracked statuses (121 findings, some split), 50 fixed, 79 open, 9 partial, 6 grown.** The fixed set concentrates in security/correctness/dependency items (the 2026-06-16 and 2026-06-21 campaigns); the open set concentrates in maintainability/documentation/DRY — consistent with a repo that fixes what bites and defers what reads.

**Finding counts (201 total):**

| Severity | Count |
|---|---|
| High | 15 |
| Medium | 84 |
| Low | 83 |
| Informational | 19 |

Actions: 58 delete · 34 extract · 30 merge · 45 simplify · 34 watch.

## 4. Owner decisions required (do not execute mechanically)

1. **strat_field dormant package (BE-3D-BUILDERS-01)** — you chose dormant-not-deleted on merge. Standing cost measured: ~290 KB, 4 integration-test files on every pre-push, the scikit-image dependency (via its dead `iso_surface.py`), and the live voxel path importing its private symbols. Recommended regardless of the keep/delete call: extract `SurfaceBound` + `_digest_surfaces` to a neutral module so the LIVE path stops depending on the dormant package. `eval_strat_builder.py` + `profile_strat_build.py` ride with this decision.
2. **C1/C2a/C2b geotech skeleton flow (BE-API-06)** — if body-centric is final, this deletes 3 endpoints, ~9 schemas, 3 use cases (BuildGeotechModelFromLitho, SuggestGeotechSubdivision, AcceptGeotechSuggestions), 3 FE fns. Geotech is the ACTIVE milestone — your call whether the ghost-flow design still has a future.
3. **DELETE /jobs/{job_id} (BE-API-02)** — the only server-side import cancel that exists; the FE's SSE abort leaves a 60 s cold-cache import running to completion server-side. Wire it into `useDovImportStream` cancel, or delete it deliberately.
4. **GeotechnicalUnit ORM + migration (BE-API-01)** — endpoints/client are deletable now; confirm debt #13 would use the model-kind path (not this table) before dropping the ORM.
5. **rewriteB Stage-2 deletion (FE-PARTITION-FILL-01)** — the code's own comments conflict (07-09 "Stage 2 deletes this" vs 07-10 "kept for A/B"). Recommended: migrate the vitest gates to rewriteB=true first (top-issue #1), then fold the flag+bridge+substrate deletion into the full-band pivot.
